Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in mendelian theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 366 and the invariant I(A,B) = 782 mod 3.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)
